        .knot-toast.info { border-left-color: var(--accent); }

            width: 34px;

            border-radius: 10px;

            place-items: center;
            background: rgba(255,255,255,0.08);

            flex-shrink: 0;
        }
        .knot-toast.success .icon-badge { color: var(--secondary); }
        .knot-toast.error .icon-badge { color: var(--danger); }
        .knot-toast.warning .icon-badge { color: var(--amber); }
        .knot-toast.info .icon-badge { color: var(--accent); }

        }

    <!-- ====================================================

        ==================================================== -->
    <div class="toast-stack" id="knotToastStack" aria-live="polite" aria-atomic="true"></div>

    <!-- ====================================================
        SCRIPTS
        ==================================================== -->
    <script>
        (function () {
            'use strict';

            /* ======================================================
            GREETING BY TIME OF DAY
            ====================================================== */
            (function setGreeting() {
                const h = new Date().getHours();
                let greeting = 'Good evening';
                if (h < 12) greeting = 'Good morning';
                else if (h < 18) greeting = 'Good afternoon';

                const h1 = document.getElementById('greetingText');
                if (h1) {
                    h1.innerHTML = greeting + ', <span>Amara</span>';
                }
            })();

            /* ======================================================
            MOBILE SIDEBAR
            ====================================================== */
            const hamburger = document.getElementById('hamburger');
            const sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');
            const navOverlay = document.getElementById('navOverlay');

            function toggleSidebar(force) {
                const open = force !== undefined ? force : !sidebar.classList.contains('open');
                sidebar.classList.toggle('open', open);
                navOverlay.classList.toggle('active', open);
                document.body.style.overflow = open ? 'hidden' : '';
            }

            if (hamburger) hamburger.addEventListener('click', function () { toggleSidebar(); });
            if (navOverlay) navOverlay.addEventListener('click', function () { toggleSidebar(false); });

            /* ======================================================
            PROFILE DROPDOWN
            ====================================================== */
            const profileBtn = document.getElementById('profileBtn');
            const profileDropdown = document.getElementById('profileDropdown');

            if (profileBtn && profileDropdown) {
                profileBtn.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    const isOpen = profileDropdown.classList.toggle('open');
                    profileBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', isOpen);
                    if (notifDropdown) {
                        notifDropdown.classList.remove('open');
                        if (notifBtn) notifBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                    }
                });
            }

            /* ======================================================
            NOTIFICATIONS DROPDOWN
            ====================================================== */
            const notifBtn = document.getElementById('notifBtn');
            const notifDropdown = document.getElementById('notifDropdown');

            if (notifBtn && notifDropdown) {
                notifBtn.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    const isOpen = notifDropdown.classList.toggle('open');
                    notifBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', isOpen);
                    if (profileDropdown) {
                        profileDropdown.classList.remove('open');
                        if (profileBtn) profileBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                    }
                });
            }

            /* Click outside → close all */
            document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                if (profileDropdown && profileBtn) {
                    if (!profileDropdown.contains(e.target) && !profileBtn.contains(e.target)) {
                        profileDropdown.classList.remove('open');
                        profileBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                    }
                }
                if (notifDropdown && notifBtn) {
                    if (!notifDropdown.contains(e.target) && !notifBtn.contains(e.target)) {
                        notifDropdown.classList.remove('open');
                        notifBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                    }
                }
            });

            /* Escape closes everything */
            document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
                if (e.key === 'Escape') {
                    if (profileDropdown) profileDropdown.classList.remove('open');
                    if (notifDropdown) notifDropdown.classList.remove('open');
                    if (profileBtn) profileBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                    if (notifBtn) notifBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                    if (sidebar && sidebar.classList.contains('open')) toggleSidebar(false);
                    document.querySelectorAll('.modal-backdrop.open').forEach(function (m) {
                        m.classList.remove('open');
                    });
                }
            });

            /* ======================================================
            MARK ALL READ
            ====================================================== */
            const markAllBtn = document.getElementById('markAllRead');
            if (markAllBtn) {
                markAllBtn.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    document.querySelectorAll('.notif-row.unread').forEach(function (r) {
                        r.classList.remove('unread');
                    });
                    const dot = notifBtn ? notifBtn.querySelector('.dot') : null;
                    if (dot) dot.style.display = 'none';
                    toast('All notifications marked as read', 'success');
                });
            }

            /* ======================================================
            TABS
            ====================================================== */
            const tabs = document.querySelectorAll('.tab');
            const panels = document.querySelectorAll('.tab-panel');

            function activateTab(name) {
                tabs.forEach(function (t) {
                    t.classList.toggle('active', t.dataset.tab === name);
                });
                panels.forEach(function (p) {
                    p.classList.toggle('active', p.id === 'tab-' + name);
                });
            }

            tabs.forEach(function (tab) {
                tab.addEventListener('click', function () {
                    activateTab(this.dataset.tab);
                });
            });

            /* ======================================================
            SIDEBAR → TAB SHORTCUTS
            ====================================================== */
            document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item[data-tab-link]').forEach(function (link) {
                link.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    const target = this.dataset.tabLink;
                    activateTab(target);

                    // Update sidebar active state
                    document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar .nav-item').forEach(function (n) {
                        n.classList.remove('active');
                    });
                    this.classList.add('active');

                    // Scroll tabs card into view
                    const card = document.querySelector('.tabs-card');
                    if (card) {
                        const offset = 90;
                        const top = card.getBoundingClientRect().top + window.pageYOffset - offset;
                        window.scrollTo({ top: top, behavior: 'smooth' });
                    }

                    if (window.innerWidth <= 900) toggleSidebar(false);
                });
            });

            /* ======================================================
            PLAIN SIDEBAR LINKS (# pages)
            ====================================================== */
            document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar .nav-item').forEach(function (link) {
                link.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                    if (this.hasAttribute('data-tab-link')) return;
                    const href = this.getAttribute('href');
                    if (href === '#') e.preventDefault();
                    document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar .nav-item').forEach(function (n) {
                        n.classList.remove('active');
                    });
                    this.classList.add('active');
                    if (window.innerWidth <= 900 && sidebar.classList.contains('open')) {
                        toggleSidebar(false);
                    }
                });
            });

            /* ======================================================
            ENTRANCE ANIMATIONS
            ====================================================== */
            const fadeEls = document.querySelectorAll('.fade-up');
            const io = new IntersectionObserver(function (entries) {
                entries.forEach(function (entry) {
                    if (entry.isIntersecting) {
                        const delay = (Array.prototype.indexOf.call(fadeEls, entry.target) % 6) * 70;
                        setTimeout(function () {
                            entry.target.classList.add('visible');
                        }, delay);
                        io.unobserve(entry.target);
                    }
                });
            }, { threshold: 0.1, rootMargin: '0px 0px -40px 0px' });

            fadeEls.forEach(function (el) { io.observe(el); });

            /* ======================================================
            COUNT-UP NUMBERS
            ====================================================== */
            const counters = document.querySelectorAll('[data-count]');
            const countObs = new IntersectionObserver(function (entries) {
                entries.forEach(function (entry) {
                    if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;
                    const el = entry.target;
                    const target = parseInt(el.dataset.count, 10) || 0;
                    const duration = 1200;
                    const start = performance.now();
                    function step(now) {
                        const p = Math.min((now - start) / duration, 1);
                        const eased = 1 - Math.pow(1 - p, 3);
                        el.textContent = Math.round(target * eased);
                        if (p < 1) requestAnimationFrame(step);
                    }
                    requestAnimationFrame(step);
                    countObs.unobserve(el);
                });
            }, { threshold: 0.5 });
            counters.forEach(function (c) { countObs.observe(c); });

            /* ======================================================
            ANIMATE BARS / CHART / RING
            ====================================================== */
            function animateBarsIn(scope) {
                const root = scope || document;
                root.querySelectorAll('.bar > span[data-value]').forEach(function (el) {
                    el.style.width = '0';
                    requestAnimationFrame(function () {
                        setTimeout(function () {
                            el.style.width = el.dataset.value + '%';
                        }, 60);
                    });
                });
            }

            window.addEventListener('load', function () {
                animateBarsIn(document);

                document.querySelectorAll('.chart .bar-v[data-h]').forEach(function (el) {
                    setTimeout(function () {
                        el.style.height = el.dataset.h + '%';
                    }, 320);
                });

                const ring = document.getElementById('ringFg');
                if (ring) {
                    const circumference = 326.73;
                    const percent = 68;
                    setTimeout(function () {
                        ring.style.strokeDashoffset = circumference - (circumference * percent / 100);
                    }, 300);
                }
            });

            /* ======================================================
            TOAST SYSTEM
            ====================================================== */
            const toastContainer = document.getElementById('knotToastStack');

            function toast(message, type) {
                if (!toastContainer || !message) return;
                const el = document.createElement('div');
                el.className = 'knot-toast ' + (type || 'success');
                const icons = {
                    success: 'bi-check-circle-fill',
                    error: 'bi-x-circle-fill',
                    warning: 'bi-exclamation-triangle-fill',
                    info: 'bi-info-circle-fill'
                };
                const labels = {
                    success: 'Success',
                    error: 'Error',
                    warning: 'Warning',
                    info: 'Info'
                };
                el.innerHTML = `
                    <span class="icon-badge"><i class="bi ${icons[type] || icons.success}"></i></span>
                    <div class="knot-toast-copy">
                        <strong>${labels[type] || 'Success'}</strong>
                        <span>${message}</span>
                    </div>
                    <button type="button" class="knot-toast-close" aria-label="Close notification"><i class="bi bi-x-lg"></i></button>
                `;
                const closeBtn = el.querySelector('.knot-toast-close');
                closeBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {
                    el.remove();
                });
                toastContainer.appendChild(el);
                setTimeout(function () { el.remove(); }, 4500);
            }
            window.__toast = toast;
            window.showKnotToast = toast;

            document.querySelectorAll('[data-toast]').forEach(function (el) {
                el.addEventListener('click', function () {
                    toast(this.dataset.toast, this.dataset.toastType);
                });
            });

            /* ======================================================
            NOTIFICATION ROW CLICK (mark as read)
            ====================================================== */
            document.querySelectorAll('.notif-row').forEach(function (row) {
                row.addEventListener('click', function () {
                    this.classList.remove('unread');
                    const txt = this.querySelector('.nt') ? this.querySelector('.nt').textContent.trim() : 'Notification';
                    console.log('Notification:', txt);
                });
            });

        })();
    </script>

    <!-- ====================================================
        DJANGO MESSAGES → TOASTS
        ==================================================== -->
    {% for message in messages %}
        <input type="hidden" class="knot-message" value="{{ message }}" data-type="{{ message.tags|default:'success' }}">
        <script>
            (function () {
                const el = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
                if (el && el.value && window.showKnotToast) {
                    window.showKnotToast(el.value, el.dataset.type || 'success');
                }
            })();
        </script>
    {% endfor %}

    <script>
        (function () {
            'use strict';

            const sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');
            const hamburger = document.getElementById('hamburger');
            const navOverlay = document.getElementById('navOverlay');
            const profileBtn = document.getElementById('profileBtn');
            const profileDropdown = document.getElementById('profileDropdown');
            const notifBtn = document.getElementById('notifBtn');
            const notifDropdown = document.getElementById('notifDropdown');

            function toggleSidebar(force) {
                const open = force !== undefined ? force : !sidebar.classList.contains('open');
                sidebar.classList.toggle('open', open);
                navOverlay.classList.toggle('active', open);
                document.body.style.overflow = open ? 'hidden' : '';
            }

            if (hamburger) hamburger.addEventListener('click', () => toggleSidebar());
            if (navOverlay) navOverlay.addEventListener('click', () => toggleSidebar(false));

            if (profileBtn) {
                profileBtn.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    const open = profileDropdown.classList.toggle('open');
                    profileBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', open);
                    if (notifDropdown) notifDropdown.classList.remove('open');
                    if (notifBtn) notifBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                });
            }

            if (notifBtn) {
                notifBtn.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    const open = notifDropdown.classList.toggle('open');
                    notifBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', open);
                    if (profileDropdown) profileDropdown.classList.remove('open');
                    if (profileBtn) profileBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                });
            }

            document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                if (profileDropdown && profileBtn && !profileDropdown.contains(e.target) && !profileBtn.contains(e.target)) {
                    profileDropdown.classList.remove('open');
                    profileBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                }
                if (notifDropdown && notifBtn && !notifDropdown.contains(e.target) && !notifBtn.contains(e.target)) {
                    notifDropdown.classList.remove('open');
                    notifBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                }
            });

            document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
                if (e.key === 'Escape') {
                    if (profileDropdown) profileDropdown.classList.remove('open');
                    if (notifDropdown) notifDropdown.classList.remove('open');
                    if (sidebar && sidebar.classList.contains('open')) toggleSidebar(false);
                }
            });

            const markAll = document.getElementById('markAllRead');
            if (markAll) {
                markAll.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    document.querySelectorAll('.notif-row.unread').forEach(r => r.classList.remove('unread'));
                    const dot = notifBtn ? notifBtn.querySelector('.dot') : null;
                    if (dot) dot.style.display = 'none';
                    toast('All notifications marked as read', 'success');
                });
            }

            document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar .nav-item').forEach(link => {
                link.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                    if (this.getAttribute('href') === '#') e.preventDefault();
                    document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar .nav-item').forEach(n => n.classList.remove('active'));
                    this.classList.add('active');
                    if (window.innerWidth <= 900 && sidebar.classList.contains('open')) toggleSidebar(false);
                });
            });

            const fadeEls = document.querySelectorAll('.fade-up');
            const io = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
                entries.forEach(entry => {
                    if (entry.isIntersecting) {
                        const delay = (Array.prototype.indexOf.call(fadeEls, entry.target) % 8) * 70;
                        setTimeout(() => entry.target.classList.add('visible'), delay);
                        io.unobserve(entry.target);
                    }
                });
            }, { threshold: 0.1, rootMargin: '0px 0px -40px 0px' });
            fadeEls.forEach(el => io.observe(el));

            const counters = document.querySelectorAll('[data-count]');
            const countObs = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
                entries.forEach(entry => {
                    if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;
                    const el = entry.target;
                    const target = parseInt(el.dataset.count, 10) || 0;
                    const duration = 1300;
                    const start = performance.now();
                    function step(now) {
                        const p = Math.min((now - start) / duration, 1);
                        const eased = 1 - Math.pow(1 - p, 3);
                        el.textContent = Math.round(target * eased);
                        if (p < 1) requestAnimationFrame(step);
                    }
                    requestAnimationFrame(step);
                    countObs.unobserve(el);
                });
            }, { threshold: 0.4 });
            counters.forEach(c => countObs.observe(c));

            window.addEventListener('load', function () {
                document.querySelectorAll('.bar > span[data-value]').forEach(el => {
                    el.style.width = '0';
                    requestAnimationFrame(() => setTimeout(() => el.style.width = el.dataset.value + '%', 80));
                });
            });

            const toastContainer = document.getElementById('toastContainer');
            function toast(message, type) {
                if (!toastContainer) return;
                const el = document.createElement('div');
                el.className = 'toast' + (type ? ' ' + type : '');
                let icon = 'bi-info-circle-fill';
                if (type === 'success') icon = 'bi-check-circle-fill';
                if (type === 'error') icon = 'bi-x-circle-fill';
                el.innerHTML = '<i class="bi ' + icon + '"></i> ' + message;
                toastContainer.appendChild(el);
                setTimeout(() => {
                    el.classList.add('out');
                    setTimeout(() => el.remove(), 400);
                }, 2800);
            }
            window.__toast = toast;

            document.querySelectorAll('[data-toast]').forEach(el => {
                el.addEventListener('click', function () {
                    toast(this.dataset.toast, this.dataset.toastType);
                });
            });

            document.querySelectorAll('.notif-row').forEach(row => {
                row.addEventListener('click', function () { this.classList.remove('unread'); });
            });
        })();
    </script>

    {% for message in messages %}
        <input type="hidden" class="django-message" value="{{ message }}" data-message-type="{{ message.tags|default:'success' }}">
        <script>
            (function () {
                var el = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
                if (el && el.value && window.__toast) {
                    window.__toast(el.value, el.dataset.messageType || 'success');
                }
            })();
        </script>
    {% endfor %}

</body>
</html>

    <script>
        (function () {
            'use strict';

            /* GREETING */
            (function setGreeting() {
                const h = new Date().getHours();
                let greeting = 'Good evening';
                if (h < 12) greeting = 'Good morning';
                else if (h < 18) greeting = 'Good afternoon';
                const h1 = document.getElementById('greetingText');
                if (h1) {
                    const name = h1.dataset.name || 'Daniel';
                    h1.innerHTML = greeting + ', <span>' + name + '</span>';
                }
            })();

            /* SIDEBAR */
            const hamburger = document.getElementById('hamburger');
            const sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');
            const navOverlay = document.getElementById('navOverlay');

            function toggleSidebar(force) {
                const open = force !== undefined ? force : !sidebar.classList.contains('open');
                sidebar.classList.toggle('open', open);
                navOverlay.classList.toggle('active', open);
                document.body.style.overflow = open ? 'hidden' : '';
            }
            if (hamburger) hamburger.addEventListener('click', () => toggleSidebar());
            if (navOverlay) navOverlay.addEventListener('click', () => toggleSidebar(false));

            /* DROPDOWNS */
            const profileBtn = document.getElementById('profileBtn');
            const profileDropdown = document.getElementById('profileDropdown');
            const notifBtn = document.getElementById('notifBtn');
            const notifDropdown = document.getElementById('notifDropdown');

            if (profileBtn && profileDropdown) {
                profileBtn.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    const isOpen = profileDropdown.classList.toggle('open');
                    profileBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', isOpen);
                    if (notifDropdown) {
                        notifDropdown.classList.remove('open');
                        if (notifBtn) notifBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                    }
                });
            }

            if (notifBtn && notifDropdown) {
                notifBtn.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    const isOpen = notifDropdown.classList.toggle('open');
                    notifBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', isOpen);
                    if (profileDropdown) {
                        profileDropdown.classList.remove('open');
                        if (profileBtn) profileBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                    }
                });
            }

            document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                if (profileDropdown && profileBtn &&
                    !profileDropdown.contains(e.target) && !profileBtn.contains(e.target)) {
                    profileDropdown.classList.remove('open');
                    profileBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                }
                if (notifDropdown && notifBtn &&
                    !notifDropdown.contains(e.target) && !notifBtn.contains(e.target)) {
                    notifDropdown.classList.remove('open');
                    notifBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                }
            });

            document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
                if (e.key === 'Escape') {
                    if (profileDropdown) profileDropdown.classList.remove('open');
                    if (notifDropdown) notifDropdown.classList.remove('open');
                    if (profileBtn) profileBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                    if (notifBtn) notifBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                    if (sidebar && sidebar.classList.contains('open')) toggleSidebar(false);
                }
            });

            /* MARK ALL READ */
            const markAllBtn = document.getElementById('markAllRead');
            if (markAllBtn) {
                markAllBtn.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    document.querySelectorAll('.notif-row.unread').forEach(r => r.classList.remove('unread'));
                    const dot = notifBtn ? notifBtn.querySelector('.dot') : null;
                    if (dot) dot.style.display = 'none';
                    toast('All notifications marked as read', 'success');
                });
            }

            /* TABS */
            const tabs = document.querySelectorAll('.tab');
            const panels = document.querySelectorAll('.tab-panel');

            function activateTab(name) {
                tabs.forEach(t => t.classList.toggle('active', t.dataset.tab === name));
                panels.forEach(p => p.classList.toggle('active', p.id === 'tab-' + name));
                animateBarsIn(document.getElementById('tab-' + name) || document);
            }
            tabs.forEach(tab => {
                tab.addEventListener('click', function () { activateTab(this.dataset.tab); });
            });

            /* SIDEBAR ACTIVE */
            document.querySelectorAll('.side-link').forEach(link => {
                link.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                    if (this.getAttribute('href') === '#') e.preventDefault();
                    document.querySelectorAll('.side-link').forEach(n => n.classList.remove('active'));
                    this.classList.add('active');
                    if (window.innerWidth <= 900 && sidebar.classList.contains('open')) toggleSidebar(false);
                });
            });

            /* ENTRANCE */
            const fadeEls = document.querySelectorAll('.fade-up');
            const io = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
                entries.forEach(entry => {
                    if (entry.isIntersecting) {
                        const delay = (Array.prototype.indexOf.call(fadeEls, entry.target) % 8) * 70;
                        setTimeout(() => entry.target.classList.add('visible'), delay);
                        io.unobserve(entry.target);
                    }
                });
            }, { threshold: 0.1, rootMargin: '0px 0px -40px 0px' });
            fadeEls.forEach(el => io.observe(el));

            /* COUNT-UP */
            const counters = document.querySelectorAll('[data-count]');
            const countObs = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
                entries.forEach(entry => {
                    if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;
                    const el = entry.target;
                    const target = parseInt(el.dataset.count, 10) || 0;
                    const duration = 1300;
                    const start = performance.now();
                    function step(now) {
                        const p = Math.min((now - start) / duration, 1);
                        const eased = 1 - Math.pow(1 - p, 3);
                        el.textContent = Math.round(target * eased);
                        if (p < 1) requestAnimationFrame(step);
                    }
                    requestAnimationFrame(step);
                    countObs.unobserve(el);
                });
            }, { threshold: 0.4 });
            counters.forEach(c => countObs.observe(c));

            /* BARS / CHART / RING */
            function animateBarsIn(scope) {
                const root = scope || document;
                root.querySelectorAll('.bar > span[data-value]').forEach(el => {
                    el.style.width = '0';
                    requestAnimationFrame(() => setTimeout(() => {
                        el.style.width = el.dataset.value + '%';
                    }, 60));
                });
            }

            window.addEventListener('load', function () {
                animateBarsIn(document);
                document.querySelectorAll('.chart .bar-v[data-h]').forEach(el => {
                    setTimeout(() => { el.style.height = el.dataset.h + '%'; }, 320);
                });
                const ring = document.getElementById('ringFg');
                if (ring) {
                    const circumference = 326.73;
                    const percent = parseInt(ring.dataset.percent || '96', 10);
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        ring.style.strokeDashoffset = circumference - (circumference * percent / 100);
                    }, 300);
                }
            });

            /* TOASTS */
            const toastContainer = document.getElementById('knotToastStack');
            function toast(message, type) {
                if (!toastContainer || !message) return;
                const el = document.createElement('div');
                el.className = 'knot-toast ' + (type || 'success');
                const icons = {
                    success: 'bi-check-circle-fill',
                    error: 'bi-x-circle-fill',
                    warning: 'bi-exclamation-triangle-fill',
                    info: 'bi-info-circle-fill'
                };
                const labels = {
                    success: 'Success',
                    error: 'Error',
                    warning: 'Warning',
                    info: 'Info'
                };
                el.innerHTML = `
                    <span class="icon-badge"><i class="bi ${icons[type] || icons.success}"></i></span>
                    <div class="knot-toast-copy">
                        <strong>${labels[type] || 'Success'}</strong>
                        <span>${message}</span>
                    </div>
                    <button type="button" class="knot-toast-close" aria-label="Close notification"><i class="bi bi-x-lg"></i></button>
                `;
                el.querySelector('.knot-toast-close').addEventListener('click', () => el.remove());
                toastContainer.appendChild(el);
                setTimeout(() => el.remove(), 4200);
            }
            window.__toast = toast;
            window.showKnotToast = toast;

            document.querySelectorAll('[data-toast]').forEach(el => {
                el.addEventListener('click', function () {
                    toast(this.dataset.toast, this.dataset.toastType);
                });
            });

            document.querySelectorAll('.notif-row').forEach(row => {
                row.addEventListener('click', function () { this.classList.remove('unread'); });
            });

        })();
    </script>

    {% for message in messages %}
        <input type="hidden" class="knot-message" value="{{ message }}" data-type="{{ message.tags|default:'success' }}">
        <script>
            (function () {
                const el = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
                if (el && el.value && window.showKnotToast) {
                    window.showKnotToast(el.value, el.dataset.type || 'success');
                }
            })();
        </script>
{% endfor %}

            letter-spacing: 0.22em; text-transform: uppercase;

    <div class="toast-stack" id="knotToastStack" aria-live="polite" aria-atomic="true"></div>

    <script>
        (function () {
            'use strict';

            /* SIDEBAR */
            const hamburger = document.getElementById('hamburger');
            const sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');
            const navOverlay = document.getElementById('navOverlay');

            function toggleSidebar(force) {
                const open = force !== undefined ? force : !sidebar.classList.contains('open');
                sidebar.classList.toggle('open', open);
                navOverlay.classList.toggle('active', open);
                document.body.style.overflow = open ? 'hidden' : '';
            }
            if (hamburger) hamburger.addEventListener('click', () => toggleSidebar());
            if (navOverlay) navOverlay.addEventListener('click', () => toggleSidebar(false));

            /* DROPDOWNS */
            const profileBtn = document.getElementById('profileBtn');
            const profileDropdown = document.getElementById('profileDropdown');
            const notifBtn = document.getElementById('notifBtn');
            const notifDropdown = document.getElementById('notifDropdown');

            function closeAllDropdowns() {
                if (profileDropdown) profileDropdown.classList.remove('open');
                if (notifDropdown) notifDropdown.classList.remove('open');
                if (profileBtn) profileBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                if (notifBtn) notifBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
            }

            if (profileBtn && profileDropdown) {
                profileBtn.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    const isOpen = profileDropdown.classList.toggle('open');
                    profileBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', isOpen);
                    if (notifDropdown) {
                        notifDropdown.classList.remove('open');
                        if (notifBtn) notifBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                    }
                });
            }

            if (notifBtn && notifDropdown) {
                notifBtn.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    const isOpen = notifDropdown.classList.toggle('open');
                    notifBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', isOpen);
                    if (profileDropdown) {
                        profileDropdown.classList.remove('open');
                        if (profileBtn) profileBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                    }
                });
            }

            document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                if (profileDropdown && profileBtn &&
                    !profileDropdown.contains(e.target) && !profileBtn.contains(e.target)) {
                    profileDropdown.classList.remove('open');
                    profileBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                }
                if (notifDropdown && notifBtn &&
                    !notifDropdown.contains(e.target) && !notifBtn.contains(e.target)) {
                    notifDropdown.classList.remove('open');
                    notifBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                }
            });

            document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
                if (e.key === 'Escape') {
                    closeAllDropdowns();
                    if (sidebar && sidebar.classList.contains('open')) toggleSidebar(false);
                }
            });

            /* MARK ALL READ */
            const markAllBtn = document.getElementById('markAllRead');
            if (markAllBtn) {
                markAllBtn.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    document.querySelectorAll('.notif-row.unread').forEach(r => r.classList.remove('unread'));
                    const dot = notifBtn ? notifBtn.querySelector('.dot') : null;
                    if (dot) dot.style.display = 'none';
                    const count = document.querySelector('.notif-count');
                    if (count) count.textContent = '0';
                    toast('All alerts marked as read', 'success');
                });
            }

            /* SIDEBAR ACTIVE */
            document.querySelectorAll('.side-link').forEach(link => {
                link.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                    if (this.getAttribute('href') === '#') e.preventDefault();
                    document.querySelectorAll('.side-link').forEach(n => n.classList.remove('active'));
                    this.classList.add('active');
                    if (window.innerWidth <= 900 && sidebar.classList.contains('open')) toggleSidebar(false);
                });
            });

            /* ENTRANCE */
            const fadeEls = document.querySelectorAll('.fade-up');
            const io = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
                entries.forEach(entry => {
                    if (entry.isIntersecting) {
                        const delay = (Array.prototype.indexOf.call(fadeEls, entry.target) % 8) * 70;
                        setTimeout(() => entry.target.classList.add('visible'), delay);
                        io.unobserve(entry.target);
                    }
                });
            }, { threshold: 0.1, rootMargin: '0px 0px -40px 0px' });
            fadeEls.forEach(el => io.observe(el));

            /* COUNT-UP */
            const counters = document.querySelectorAll('[data-count]');
            const countObs = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
                entries.forEach(entry => {
                    if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;
                    const el = entry.target;
                    const target = parseInt(el.dataset.count, 10) || 0;
                    const duration = 1300;
                    const start = performance.now();
                    function step(now) {
                        const p = Math.min((now - start) / duration, 1);
                        const eased = 1 - Math.pow(1 - p, 3);
                        el.textContent = Math.round(target * eased);
                        if (p < 1) requestAnimationFrame(step);
                    }
                    requestAnimationFrame(step);
                    countObs.unobserve(el);
                });
            }, { threshold: 0.4 });
            counters.forEach(c => countObs.observe(c));

            /* ANIMATE BARS */
            function animateBarsIn(scope) {
                const root = scope || document;
                root.querySelectorAll('.bar > span[data-value]').forEach(el => {
                    el.style.width = '0';
                    requestAnimationFrame(() => setTimeout(() => {
                        el.style.width = el.dataset.value + '%';
                    }, 60));
                });
            }

            window.addEventListener('load', function () {
                animateBarsIn(document);
            });

            /* TOASTS */
            const toastContainer = document.getElementById('knotToastStack');
            function toast(message, type) {
                if (!toastContainer || !message) return;
                const el = document.createElement('div');
                el.className = 'knot-toast ' + (type || 'info');
                const icons = {
                    success: 'bi-check-circle-fill',
                    error: 'bi-x-octagon-fill',
                    warning: 'bi-exclamation-triangle-fill',
                    info: 'bi-info-circle-fill',
                    violet: 'bi-shield-lock-fill'
                };
                const labels = {
                    success: 'Success',
                    error: 'Critical',
                    warning: 'Notice',
                    info: 'Info',
                    violet: 'Admin'
                };
                el.innerHTML = `
                    <span class="icon-badge"><i class="bi ${icons[type] || icons.info}"></i></span>
                    <div class="knot-toast-copy">
                        <strong>${labels[type] || 'Info'}</strong>
                        <span>${message}</span>
                    </div>
                    <button type="button" class="knot-toast-close" aria-label="Close notification"><i class="bi bi-x-lg"></i></button>
                `;
                el.querySelector('.knot-toast-close').addEventListener('click', () => el.remove());
                toastContainer.appendChild(el);
                setTimeout(() => el.remove(), 4500);
            }
            window.__toast = toast;
            window.showKnotToast = toast;

            document.querySelectorAll('[data-toast]').forEach(el => {
                el.addEventListener('click', function () {
                    toast(this.dataset.toast, this.dataset.toastType);
                });
            });

            /* NOTIFICATION ROW CLICK */
            document.querySelectorAll('.notif-row').forEach(row => {
                row.addEventListener('click', function () { this.classList.remove('unread'); });
            });

        })();
    </script>

    {% for message in messages %}
        <input type="hidden" class="knot-message" value="{{ message }}" data-type="{{ message.tags|default:'info' }}">
        <script>
            (function () {
                const el = document.currentScript.previousElementSibling;
                if (el && el.value && window.showKnotToast) {
                    window.showKnotToast(el.value, el.dataset.type || 'info');
                }
            })();
        </script>
    {% endfor %}

    {% block extra_js %}{% endblock %}

</body>
</html>

approved, rejected

on_delete=models.SET_NULL, related_name='reviews')
created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

* `Knot_Teacher/studio.html`
* `Knot_Student/sessions.html`
* `Knot_Mentor/sessions.html`
* `LearningSession`
* `SessionHand`
* `StudioRecording`
* `SessionAttendance`
* `SessionNote`
* the related Django views and session actions.
And I think this is an area where KnotWork can become **much more
ambitious**.
### The important distinction
Your current Teacher Studio is already a **very good UI prototype**. It has:
* live-session header
* session timer
* recording controls
* camera/screen/both modes
* participant rail
* raised-hand interaction
* recording library
* upload area
* room/device setup
* session scheduling
* session ending
* session notes
* session roster
Your Student Sessions page also has a good foundation:
* upcoming sessions
* past sessions
* cancelled sessions
* join
* reschedule
* cancel
* rebook
* recordings
* session history
* calendar
* session tips
But for a **global-scale learning platform**, these are currently closer to
**"excellent dashboard simulations"** than a truly world-class learning
environment.
For example, the Teacher Studio contains hard-coded/demo values such as
**24 attendees, 92% engagement, Shure MV7, 842 Mbps, Sara Kim**, etc.
Those need to become real-time platform data.
More importantly, `LearningSession` currently has a single `student`
relationship. That will become restrictive for group classes, cohorts,
workshops and large live lessons.
## What I would turn it into
I would make the system have **three separate experiences**:
### 1. Teacher Studio
The teacher's professional control center.
### 2. Student Learning Room
The student's actual learning environment.
### 3. Shared Live Classroom
The real-time environment both enter when they click **Join Session**.
That third part is the biggest missing piece.
Instead of:
> Student → Sessions → Join
and
> Teacher → Studio → Go Live
you want:
> **Teacher Studio → Launch Classroom ← Student Learning Room**
Both users enter the **same live classroom**, but with completely different
permissions and interfaces.
---

# 22. Global-scale architecture
For what you're describing, I would eventually separate the live infrastructure
from normal Django page rendering.
Something like:
```text
 KNOTWORK
 │
┌──────────────┼──────────────┐
│  │  │
Django Realtime Storage
│  │  │
Authentication WebRTC/SFU Video files
Users WebSockets Recordings
Courses Presence Documents
Payments Chat Images
Progress Reactions Resources
│  │
└──────────────┼──────────────┘
 │
 AI Services
 │
 Transcript / Summary
 Recommendations
 Learning Assistant
```
For the actual live media layer, don't try to build a global video infrastructure
from scratch with Django.
Use a proper **WebRTC/SFU infrastructure** and let Django manage the
application/business layer.
---
# 23. One major architectural change I'd make now
Your current system has:
```text
Teacher
Mentor
Student
```
KnotWork's philosophy is **mutual learning**.
So eventually a person shouldn't be permanently "just a student".
Someone can be:
> Student in React
and simultaneously:
> Teacher in Photoshop
and:
> Mentor in Career Development.
That's a very important architectural concept for KnotWork.
The platform should think in terms of:
```text
User
 │
├── Learner identity
├── Teacher identity
├── Mentor identity
└── Skill identity
```
rather than locking the person into one permanent role.

### The biggest thing I would NOT do
I wouldn't simply keep adding buttons to your existing `studio.html`.
That will eventually become a giant dashboard full of controls.
Instead, turn KnotWork into **three coordinated products inside one
platform**:
**Teacher Studio**
→ prepares, teaches, monitors and analyzes.
**Student Learning Room**
→ learns, participates, practices and reflects.
**KnotWork Live Classroom**
→ the real-time shared environment connecting both.
That would give you a much stronger foundation for the kind of **global
online learning ecosystem** you're aiming for.
I can next turn your existing KnotWork code into a **complete Teacher Studio
+ Student Learning Room + Live Classroom specification**, including the
exact pages, components, Django models, realtime events, permissions, and
UI layout for each screen.
You just described the exact difference between a good LMS and a worldclass one. You are 100% right.
Your current `studio.html` and `sessions.html` are perfect *dashboard
prototypes* - but KnotWork needs to be 3 products, not 1 dashboard.
Let's lock this architecture now. This is how we fix your ZIP.
The Core Fix: 3 Experiences, Not 2
Right now you have:
`Teacher -> Studio -> Go Live` and `Student -> Sessions -> Join` - two
separate flows.
We change to:
*`Teacher Studio -> LAUNCH -> Shared Live Classroom <- JOIN <- Student
Learning Room`*
Same `roomId`, same WebRTC SFU, but two completely different UIs with
different permissions.
1. Your Django Models MUST Evolve Now
This is your biggest blocker. `LearningSession.student = ForeignKey` will kill
you for cohorts.
Replace it with this:
# THE NEW CORE
class LearningSession(models.Model):
id = UUID
title, course, teacher
status = Draft -> Scheduled -> Preparing -> WaitingRoom -> Live -> Ending
-> Processing -> Completed -> AssetsGenerated
type = ONE_TO_ONE | GROUP | COHORT | WORKSHOP
scheduled_at, duration
# No more single student
class SessionParticipant(models.Model):
session = FK(LearningSession)
user = FK(User) # not Student - use User
role = HOST | CO_HOST | STUDENT | OBSERVER
joined_at, left_at
attendance = PRESENT | LATE | ABSENT
hand_raised = bool
mic_enabled, cam_enabled
class SessionQuestion(models.Model): # Your own Q&A system, not chat
session = FK, author = FK, text, upvotes,
status=UNANSWERED/ANSWERED/RESOLVED, is_pinned
class SessionPoll + SessionPollResponse
class SessionActivity # for Whiteboard, Code, etc
class SessionRecording # Expand with transcript, chapters, captions,
summary
class SessionTranscript # with timestamps for click-to-seek
class SessionNote # private per user
This instantly solves: group classes, 1 teacher teaching React while learning
Photoshop, and your KnotWork philosophy of mutual learning.
2. What We Build Next - In Order
*Phase 1: The Missing Piece - KnotWork Live Classroom*
This is what you called out. Don't add more buttons to `studio.html`.
Build `/live/[roomId]` - the shared engine:
- *Main Stage* that switches: Camera | Screen | Presentation | Whiteboard |
Code Editor | Document | Media
- *Subject-Aware Dock:* If `course.type == CODING`, show
Code/Terminal/Preview. If MATH, show Whiteboard/Equation/Graph. This is
your moat.
- *Waiting Room Check:* Camera ✓ Mic ✓ Speaker ✓ Internet 842 Mbps ✓ -
"Teacher will be here in 4 min" + Today's Objective
*Phase 2: Upgrade Teacher Studio to Command Center*
Keep your UI but replace fake data:
- `24 attendees, 92%, Shure MV7` -> Real-time from `SessionParticipant` +
`navigator.mediaDevices`
- Right rail becomes: Participants (with engagement, connection, spotlight,
remove) | Questions (7 unanswered
) | Polls (Create Poll) | AI Copilot
*Phase 3: Upgrade Student to Learning Room*
- `/student/sessions` stays as management page (your current good
foundation)
- New page `/student/learn/[sessionId]` when they click Join:
Main Stage + Student Toolbar (Raise Hand, Reactions, Captions, Notes) +
Your Learning Panel (Objective, Progress 80%, My Notes, Resources)
*Phase 4: Post-Session Intelligence*
After `Live -> Ending`:
Auto pipeline: Recording -> Transcription -> Chapters with timestamps -> AI
Summary -> Homework
- Student sees: Session Complete 47m, You learned ✓ useReducer,
Participation 4 Qs, Continue: Watch recording, Practice
- Teacher sees: Attendance 24/26, Questions 31/27 answered, Students
needing follow-up
3. Global Architecture You Need
Don't run video through Django.
Django (Auth, Courses, Payments, Progress)
+
LiveKit / Cloudflare Calls (SFU for WebRTC, WebSockets for
Questions/Polls/Reactions)
+
S3 / Cloudflare R2 (Recordings, Thumbnails, Transcripts)
+
AI Service (Whisper for captions, LLM for Summary/Student Assistant)

1. NEW `models.py` - Replace your old ones
Create new app `live` -> `live/models.py`
import uuid
from django.db import models
from django.conf import settings
class LearningSession(models.Model):
class Status(models.TextChoices):
DRAFT = 'DRAFT', 'Draft'
SCHEDULED = 'SCHEDULED', 'Scheduled'
PREPARING = 'PREPARING', 'Preparing'
WAITING_ROOM = 'WAITING_ROOM', 'Waiting Room'
LIVE = 'LIVE', 'Live'
ENDING = 'ENDING', 'Ending'
PROCESSING = 'PROCESSING', 'Processing'
COMPLETED = 'COMPLETED', 'Completed'
ASSETS_GENERATED = 'ASSETS_GENERATED', 'Assets Generated'
class Type(models.TextChoices):
ONE_TO_ONE = 'ONE_TO_ONE', '1-on-1'
GROUP = 'GROUP', 'Group'
COHORT = 'COHORT', 'Cohort'
WORKSHOP = 'WORKSHOP', 'Workshop'
id = models.UUIDField(primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4,
editable=False)
title = models.CharField(max_length=255) # e.g. React Hooks Deep Dive -
Session 8
course = models.ForeignKey('courses.Course',
on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True)
teacher = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='taught_sessions')
type = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=Type.choices,
default=Type.GROUP)
status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=Status.choices,
default=Status.DRAFT)
scheduled_at = models.DateTimeField()
duration_minutes = models.IntegerField(default=45)
room_id = models.CharField(max_length=100, unique=True,
default=uuid.uuid4) # for LiveKit
# For Subject-Aware Dock
subject_mode = models.CharField(max_length=20, default='GENERAL') #
CODING, MATH, LANGUAGE, DESIGN
created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
class SessionParticipant(models.Model):
class Role(models.TextChoices):
HOST = 'HOST', 'Host'
CO_HOST = 'CO_HOST', 'Co-host'
STUDENT = 'STUDENT', 'Student'
OBSERVER = 'OBSERVER', 'Observer'
id = models.UUIDField(primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4,
editable=False)
session = models.ForeignKey(LearningSession,
on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='participants')
user = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
on_delete=models.CASCADE)
role = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=Role.choices,
default=Role.STUDENT)
joined_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
left_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
attendance = models.CharField(max_length=20, default='INVITED') #
INVITED, PRESENT, LATE, ABSENT
hand_raised = models.BooleanField(default=False)
hand_raised_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
is_muted = models.BooleanField(default=True)
is_cam_off = models.BooleanField(default=True)
class SessionQuestion(models.Model):
session = models.ForeignKey(LearningSession,
on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='questions')
author = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
on_delete=models.CASCADE)
text = models.TextField()
upvotes = models.IntegerField(default=0)
status = models.CharField(max_length=20, default='UNANSWERED') #
UNANSWERED, ANSWERED, RESOLVED
is_pinned = models.BooleanField(default=False)
created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
class SessionPoll(models.Model):
session = models.ForeignKey(LearningSession,
on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='polls')
question = models.CharField(max_length=500)
options = models.JSONField() # ["Redux", "Context", "Zustand"]
is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)
created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
class SessionPollResponse(models.Model):
poll = models.ForeignKey(SessionPoll, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
related_name='responses')
user = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
on_delete=models.CASCADE)
selected_option = models.IntegerField()
class SessionRecording(models.Model):
session = models.OneToOneField(LearningSession,
on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='recording')
video_url = models.URLField(null=True)
thumbnail_url = models.URLField(null=True)
duration_seconds = models.IntegerField(null=True)
size_mb = models.FloatField(null=True)
status = models.CharField(max_length=20, default='PROCESSING') #
PROCESSING, READY, FAILED
transcript_url = models.URLField(null=True)
summary = models.TextField(null=True) # AI summary
chapters = models.JSONField(null=True) # [{"time": "12:43", "title":
"useReducer"}]
views = models.IntegerField(default=0)
class SessionNote(models.Model):
session = models.ForeignKey(LearningSession,
on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='notes')
user = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
on_delete=models.CASCADE)
content = models.TextField()
is_private = models.BooleanField(default=True) # Student private reflection
timestamp_seconds = models.IntegerField(null=True) # note at 12:43 in
video
created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
class SessionTranscript(models.Model):
session = models.ForeignKey(LearningSession,
on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name='transcripts')
speaker = models.ForeignKey(settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL,
on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True)
text = models.TextField()
start_seconds = models.FloatField()
end_seconds = models.FloatField()
Run: `python manage.py makemigrations && migrate`
2. THE 3 PAGES SPEC
*A. /teacher/studio - Control Center*
Top bar: Session Title | Status LIVE | Duration 00:07:55 | Connection Excellent
| REC | Participants 24 | AI Copilot | Settings | End Session
Main: Preview of classroom + 3 cards you already have (Record, Upload,
Room Setup)
Right: Scheduled Sessions list (from LearningSession where
status=SCHEDULED)
*B. /live/[roomId] - Shared Classroom (MOST IMPORTANT)*
This is ONE React component but renders differently:
If `role == HOST`:
Bottom Dock: Mic | Cam | Share Screen | Present | Whiteboard | Code | Chat |
Participants | Questions (7
) | Polls | Activities | Resources | Notes | AI |
Recording | Settings
Right Panel: Participants (mute/spotlight/remove) | Questions | Polls | AI
Summary
If `role == STUDENT`:
Bottom Dock: Mic | Cam | Raise Hand (BIG LIME) | Chat | Reactions | Captions
| Leave
Right Panel: Tabs - Chat | Q&A (ask + upvote) | My Notes (private) |

*C. /student/sessions - Management + Learning Room*
Keep your current design as management page. When click Join -> Go to
`/live/[roomId]?role=student`
3. Realtime Events - For http://Socket.io / LiveKit
student:hand_raised -> { userId, sessionId } -> teacher sees HAND badge
teacher:unmute_student -> { userId }
teacher:create_poll -> { question, options } -> all students see poll
student:submit_poll -> { pollId, option }
student:ask_question -> { text } -> teacher sees in Questions panel
student:upvote_question -> { questionId }
teacher:switch_stage -> { mode: 'WHITEBOARD' | 'CODE' | 'SCREEN' }
session:status_changed -> SCHEDULED -> WAITING_ROOM -> LIVE
4. Permission Logic
def can_go_live(user, session):
return user == session.teacher and session.status == 'SCHEDULED' and
within_10_mins(session.scheduled_at)
def can_end_session(user, session):
return user == session.teacher or participant.role == 'CO_HOST'
def can_share_screen(user, session):
if user == session.teacher: return True
return session.participants.filter(user=user, role__in=
['CO_HOST','STUDENT']).exists() and session.type != 'COHORT' # etc
